Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me compile my findings:

## Report: MCP List & Panorama Pages Analysis

### 1. **MCP List Page** (`/app/pages/extensions/index.vue`)
**Path:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/extensions/index.vue`

**Current Structure:**
- Simple header with "Browse Extensions" title + count
- FilterBar component for faceted search (creators, publishers, tags)
- **McpPanoramaBanner component** conditionally rendered when `category === "mcp"` filter is active
- ExtGrid displaying filtered extension items

**Panorama Entry Point Location:**
The link is placed as a **contextual banner between the FilterBar and ExtGrid** — visible only when viewing MCP extensions. This is an elegant progressive disclosure pattern (commit `db5af3d` emphasized "contextual" banner).

**Banner Styling:**
- Lucide icon (LayoutGrid) in accent-colored rounded container
- Title + subtitle text with truncation safety
- Chevron CTA on the right, fully interactive with link styling
- Tailwind: `rounded-lg border border-(--color-accent)/25 bg-(--color-accent)/8` with hover state lifting

**i18n Keys Used:**
- `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.title`
- `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.subtitle`
- `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.cta`

---

### 2. **MCP Panorama Page** (`/app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue`)
**Path:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue`
**Layout:** Uses custom `mcp-panorama.vue` layout (minimal TopBar only, no AgentCenter sidebar)

**Current Structure:**
- **LayerSidebar** (left): Collapsible tree showing Public/Industry layer groups with drill-down interactivity
- **SectionHeader** (top-right): 
  - Large serif title (36px) with optional breadcrumb + "Clear" button
  - Subtitle with tool counts
  - Panorama/List view toggle buttons (small pill-style UI)
  - Status filter chips (All, Released, In Dev, No MCP needed)
  - Search input with icon
- **PanoramaView** (main): Responsive grid of category cards (sector/domain-based, 320px–520px min-width)
- **ToolDetailPanel** (right slide-out): Shows details for selected tool

**Visual Hierarchy:**
- Serif (font-serif) for primary stats/titles (36px, 28px)
- Mono (font-mono) for labels, status badges, percentages
- Sans for supporting text
- Color-coded status indicators (dots + full-width bar in LayerSummary)

**Key Components & Styling Patterns:**
- **LayerSummary**: Grid layout with stacked bar chart (status proportions), leading/lagging callouts
- **SectorCard**: Horizontal grid with big left panel + 4 narrow stat columns, detailed status breakdown
- **StatusChip**: Interactive filter buttons with count badges
- **Search & Filters**: Proper focus states, icon overlays, form-like styling

**Data-Driven Content:**
- Fetches `/api/internal/mcp-landscape` per layer
- Computes filtered groups, stats, visible counts (reflecting drill-down + search)
- Real-time search across name, nameZh, blurb, blurbZh, tags

**i18n Keys Used:**
- `mcpPanorama.layer.{public|industry|industryShort}`
- `mcpPanorama.page.title`, `errorLoad`, `retry`
- `mcpPanorama.page.subtitleAll`, `subtitleScoped`
- `mcpPanorama.filter.{all|clear|searchPlaceholder}`
- `mcpPanorama.status.{released|dev|none}.{label|short}`
- `mcpPanorama.view.{panorama|list}`
- `mcpPanorama.summary.*` (groupsCount, mcpActive, released, inDev, noMcp, leading, lagging)

---

### **Observations on Polish & Patterns to Reuse**

**Unpolished Elements:**
1. **SectionHeader layout is cramped**: Status chips, search, and view toggle all squeeze into flex row — wraps awkwardly on smaller screens (breakpoint missing)
2. **Missing visual hierarchy between sections**: LayerSummary and cards have no breathing room distinction; could use subtle spacing or divider
3. **ToolDetailPanel lacks visual anchor**: Slides in from right but no indication of what closes it or visual hierarchy to main content
4. **Breadcrumb styling is minimal**: Font-mono, tiny, easy to miss; could pop with accent color on the layer portion

**Elegant Patterns Evident Elsewhere:**
- **Home page hero** (`index.vue`): Uses gradient card + serif + description + CTA pattern; could elevate Panorama breadcrumb similarly
- **LayerSummary's color-coded bars**: Status indicators use consistent design tokens (--color-status-*) and proportional widths — reusable pattern
- **Banner contextual placement**: The McpPanoramaBanner is perfectly positioned; reuse for other cross-page CTAs
- **Font mixing discipline**: Serif for stats/big numbers, mono for tags/labels, sans for body — consistent throughout

---

### **Summary**
- **List page**: Minimal, clean; banner is the only entry point (smart contextual placement)
- **Panorama page**: Data-rich, interactive, well-structured but slightly crowded in the header
- **Key i18n terms**: ~15 keys to localize (mostly in `mcpPanorama.*` and `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.*`)
- **Design tokens**: Relies on Editorial Ivory theme (--color-accent, --color-card, --color-border) + layer-specific colors (--color-layer-{public|industry}-*)

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import type { McpStatus } from "~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape"
3	import type { Group, Layer, LayerPayload, ToolDto } from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
4	
5	definePageMeta({ layout: "mcp-panorama" })
6	
7	const { t } = useI18n()
8	const head = useLocaleHead()
9	useHead(() => ({
10	  title: t("mcpPanorama.page.title"),
11	  htmlAttrs: head.value.htmlAttrs ?? {},
12	}))
13	
14	const layer = ref<Layer>("public")
15	const activePrimary = ref<string | null>(null)
16	const activeSecondary = ref<string | null>(null)
17	const statusFilter = ref<"all" | McpStatus>("all")
18	const search = ref("")
19	const viewMode = ref<"panorama" | "list">("panorama")
20	const activeTool = ref<ToolDto | null>(null)
21	
22	// Re-fetch when layer changes; everything else filters client-side.
23	const { data, pending, error, refresh } = await useFetch<LayerPayload>(
24	  "/api/internal/mcp-landscape",
25	  {
26	    query: { layer },
27	    key: "mcp-landscape",
28	  },
29	)
30	
31	watch(layer, () => {
32	  activePrimary.value = null
33	  activeSecondary.value = null
34	  activeTool.value = null
35	})
36	
37	// All tools for the current layer (used for derived counts).
38	const allTools = computed<ToolDto[]>(() =>
39	  data.value ? data.value.groups.flatMap((g) => g.items) : [],
40	)
41	
42	// Search match — single source of truth, used by both filterTool (which
43	// drives what's rendered) and visibleCounts (which drives the chip badges).
44	function matchesSearch(tool: ToolDto): boolean {
45	  const q = search.value.trim().toLowerCase()
46	  if (!q) return true
47	  return (
48	    tool.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)
49	    || (tool.nameZh ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(q)
50	    || tool.blurb.toLowerCase().includes(q)
51	    || tool.blurbZh.toLowerCase().includes(q)
52	    || tool.tags.some((tg) => tg.toLowerCase().includes(q))
53	  )
54	}
55	
56	// Filter individual tools (drill-down + status + search).
57	function filterTool(tool: ToolDto): boolean {
58	  if (activePrimary.value && tool.ownerPrimary !== activePrimary.value) return false
59	  if (activeSecondary.value && tool.ownerSecondary !== activeSecondary.value) return false
60	  if (statusFilter.value !== "all" && tool.status !== statusFilter.value) return false
61	  return matchesSearch(tool)
62	}
63	
64	// Re-shape groups with filtered items, dropping empty groups/PDTs.
65	const filteredGroups = computed<Group[]>(() => {
66	  if (!data.value) return []
67	  return data.value.groups
68	    .map((g): Group => {
69	      const items = g.items.filter(filterTool)
70	      if (g.kind === "sector") {
71	        return { ...g, items, stats: computeStats(items) }
72	      }
73	      const pdts = g.pdts
74	        .map((p) => ({ ...p, items: p.items.filter(filterTool) }))
75	        .filter((p) => p.items.length > 0)
76	      return { ...g, items, pdts, stats: computeStats(items) }
77	    })
78	    .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0)
79	})
80	
81	function computeStats(items: ToolDto[]) {
82	  const counts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0 }
83	  for (const it of items) counts[it.status]++
84	  const total = items.length
85	  if (total === 0) {
86	    return { total, counts, releasedPct: 0, activePct: 0, lagPct: 0 }
87	  }
88	  return {
89	    total,
90	    counts,
91	    releasedPct: Math.round((counts.released / total) * 100),
92	    activePct: Math.round(((counts.released + counts.dev) / total) * 100),
93	    lagPct: Math.round((counts.none / total) * 100),
94	  }
95	}
96	
97	// Visible counts for the section header subtitle and filter chips. These
98	// reflect search + drill-down but ignore the status filter — chips show how
99	// many tools each status would surface if selected.
100	const visibleCounts = computed(() => {
101	  const counts = { released: 0, dev: 0, none: 0, total: 0 }
102	  if (!data.value) return counts
103	  for (const tool of allTools.value) {
104	    if (activePrimary.value && tool.ownerPrimary !== activePrimary.value) continue
105	    if (activeSecondary.value && tool.ownerSecondary !== activeSecondary.value) continue
106	    if (!matchesSearch(tool)) continue
107	    counts[tool.status]++
108	    counts.total++
109	  }
110	  return counts
111	})
112	
113	const totals = computed(() => ({ total: data.value?.layerStats.total ?? 0 }))
114	
115	function setActive(primary: string | null, secondary: string | null) {
116	  activePrimary.value = primary
117	  activeSecondary.value = secondary
118	  activeTool.value = null
119	}
120	
121	function clearDrill() {
122	  activePrimary.value = null
123	  activeSecondary.value = null
124	}
125	
126	function pickTool(tool: ToolDto) {
127	  activeTool.value = tool
128	}
129	</script>
130	
131	<template>
132	  <div class="contents">
133	    <!-- Sidebar -->
134	    <ClientOnly>
135	    <LayerSidebar
136	      v-if="data"
137	      :layer="layer"
138	      :groups="data.groups"
139	      :total-count="totals.total"
140	      :active-primary="activePrimary"
141	      :active-secondary="activeSecondary"
142	      @update:layer="(l: Layer) => (layer = l)"
143	      @set-active="setActive"
144	    />
145	    <template #fallback>
146	      <div class="w-[268px] shrink-0 border-r border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-sidebar)" />
147	    </template>
148	  </ClientOnly>
149	
150	  <!-- Main + side panel -->
151	  <div class="flex-1 overflow-auto bg-(--color-bg) relative">
152	    <SectionHeader
153	      v-if="data"
154	      :layer="layer"
155	      :active-primary="activePrimary"
156	      :active-secondary="activeSecondary"
157	      :visible-counts="visibleCounts"
158	      :totals="totals"
159	      :status-filter="statusFilter"
160	      :view-mode="viewMode"
161	      :search="search"
162	      :groups="data.groups"
163	      @update:status-filter="(v: 'all' | McpStatus) => (statusFilter = v)"
164	      @update:view-mode="(v: 'panorama' | 'list') => (viewMode = v)"
165	      @update:search="(v: string) => (search = v)"
166	      @clear-drill="clearDrill"
167	    />
168	
169	    <div v-if="pending && !data" class="px-7 pb-7 pt-2 text-(--color-ink-muted)">
170	      <div class="h-6 w-40 rounded bg-(--color-border) animate-pulse" />
171	    </div>
172	
173	    <div v-if="error" class="px-7 pb-7 pt-2">
174	      <div class="text-(--color-ink-muted) text-[13px] mb-2">{{ t("mcpPanorama.page.errorLoad") }}</div>
175	      <button
176	        type="button"
177	        class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg) text-[12px] font-medium cursor-pointer"
178	        @click="refresh()"
179	      >
180	        {{ t("mcpPanorama.page.retry") }}
181	      </button>
182	    </div>
183	
184	    <PanoramaView
185	      v-if="data && viewMode === 'panorama'"
186	      :layer="layer"
187	      :stats="!activePrimary && !activeSecondary && statusFilter === 'all' && !search.trim()
188	        ? data.layerStats
189	        : computeStats(filteredGroups.flatMap((g) => g.items))"
190	      :groups="filteredGroups"
191	      :active-id="activeTool?.id ?? null"
192	      @pick="pickTool"
193	    />
194	    <GroupedListView
195	      v-else-if="data && viewMode === 'list'"
196	      :layer="layer"
197	      :groups="filteredGroups"
198	      :active-id="activeTool?.id ?? null"
199	      @pick="pickTool"
200	    />
201	  </div>
202	
203	    <ToolDetailPanel
204	      :tool="activeTool"
205	      :groups="data?.groups ?? []"
206	      @close="activeTool = null"
207	    />
208	  </div>
209	</template>

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { LayoutGrid, List, Search } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import type { McpStatus } from "~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape"
4	import {
5	  groupDisplayTitle,
6	  pdtDisplayTitle,
7	  STATUS_ORDER,
8	  type Group,
9	  type Layer,
10	  type PdtBlock,
11	} from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
12	import StatusChip from "./StatusChip.vue"
13	
14	const props = defineProps<{
15	  layer: Layer
16	  activePrimary: string | null
17	  activeSecondary: string | null
18	  visibleCounts: { total: number; released: number; dev: number; none: number }
19	  totals: { total: number }
20	  statusFilter: "all" | McpStatus
21	  viewMode: "panorama" | "list"
22	  search: string
23	  groups: Group[]
24	}>()
25	
26	const emit = defineEmits<{
27	  "update:statusFilter": ["all" | McpStatus]
28	  "update:viewMode": ["panorama" | "list"]
29	  "update:search": [string]
30	  clearDrill: []
31	}>()
32	
33	const { locale, t } = useI18n()
34	
35	const layerLabel = computed(() => t(`mcpPanorama.layer.${props.layer}`))
36	
37	const titleAndCrumb = computed<{ title: string; crumb: string | null }>(() => {
38	  if (!props.activePrimary) {
39	    return { title: t("mcpPanorama.layer." + props.layer), crumb: null }
40	  }
41	  const primary = props.groups.find((g) => g.key === props.activePrimary)
42	  if (!primary) return { title: props.activePrimary, crumb: layerLabel.value }
43	  if (props.layer === "industry") {
44	    return {
45	      title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value),
46	      crumb: layerLabel.value,
47	    }
48	  }
49	  if (primary.kind !== "domain") {
50	    return { title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value), crumb: layerLabel.value }
51	  }
52	  if (props.activeSecondary) {
53	    const pdt = primary.pdts.find((p) => p.key === props.activeSecondary) as PdtBlock | undefined
54	    if (pdt) {
55	      return {
56	        title: pdtDisplayTitle(pdt, locale.value),
57	        crumb: `${layerLabel.value} / ${groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value)}`,
58	      }
59	    }
60	  }
61	  return {
62	    title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value),
63	    crumb: layerLabel.value,
64	  }
65	})
66	
67	const subtitle = computed(() => {
68	  if (!props.activePrimary) {
69	    return t("mcpPanorama.page.subtitleAll", {
70	      visible: props.visibleCounts.total,
71	      total: props.totals.total,
72	    })
73	  }
74	  return t("mcpPanorama.page.subtitleScoped", {
75	    visible: props.visibleCounts.total,
76	    released: props.visibleCounts.released,
77	    dev: props.visibleCounts.dev,
78	    none: props.visibleCounts.none,
79	  })
80	})
81	
82	function statusLabel(s: McpStatus): string {
83	  return t(`mcpPanorama.status.${s}.label`)
84	}
85	
86	const localSearch = computed({
87	  get: () => props.search,
88	  set: (v: string) => emit("update:search", v),
89	})
90	</script>

> AGENT

Here's what I'm seeing in the current state and where I'd focus the polish.

**MCP List page banner** (`app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue`)
The banner only appears when `category=mcp` is active — it's a single icon-tile + two-line CTA. Already tidy, but it reads more like a UI nudge than a destination link. It lacks any "preview" of what awaits (no count, no visual hint of sectors/layers), and most visitors of `/extensions` never see it because it's filter-gated.

**MCP Panorama page header** (`app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue`)
The header tries to fit five things on two rows: breadcrumb, title, subtitle, view toggle (`Panorama | List`), status chips, search. The view toggle visually competes with the title; the search input shares a row with status chips (`ml-auto`) and wraps awkwardly when chips grow; the breadcrumb's "Clear" button is a tiny mono text link easy to miss. There's also no visual rest between header and content — no divider, no spacing rhythm.

**Panorama page overall** (`app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue`)
First-time visitors land directly in dense data with no framing — no one-line "what is this" intro, no soft entry. The detail panel slides in without a clear visual anchor.

My instinct: three small, coherent moves —
1. Elevate the banner with a stat hint (e.g. "240 tools · 12 sectors · 2 layers") + a richer visual treatment (subtle gradient + decorative chip motif), and make it visible on the extensions index whenever there's no active query (not just when `category=mcp`).
2. Reorganize `SectionHeader` into two clean tiers: a **title tier** (breadcrumb + title + subtitle, view toggle pushed to the right but visually quieter), and a **filter tier** (status chips left, search right, with a divider hairline between tiers).
3. Add a slim intro strip on the panorama page first-load (replaced by drill-down breadcrumb once a sector is selected) so the page has a confident opening.

Let me confirm direction with a couple of questions before I commit to a final plan.

2	import { ArrowRight, Factory, Globe2, Link2, X } from "lucide-vue-next"

8	} from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"

10	
11	const props = defineProps<{
12	  tool: ToolDto | null
13	  groups: Group[]
14	}>()
15	
16	const emit = defineEmits<{ close: [] }>()
17	
18	const { locale, t } = useI18n()
19	const localePath = useLocalePath()
20	
21	const open = computed(() => props.tool !== null)
22	
23	const displayName = computed(() => (props.tool ? toolDisplayName(props.tool, locale.value) : ""))
24	const displayBlurb = computed(() => (props.tool ? toolDisplayBlurb(props.tool, locale.value) : ""))
25	
26	const ownerSummary = computed(() => {
27	  if (!props.tool) return ""
28	  const g = props.groups.find((x) => x.key === props.tool!.ownerPrimary)
29	  if (!g) return props.tool.ownerPrimary
30	  const primaryLabel = locale.value === "zh" ? g.labelZh : g.label
31	  if (props.tool.ownerSecondary && g.kind === "domain") {
32	    const pdt = g.pdts.find((p) => p.key === props.tool!.ownerSecondary)
33	    if (pdt) {
34	      const pdtLabel = locale.value === "zh" ? pdt.labelZh : pdt.label
35	      return `${primaryLabel} · ${pdtLabel}`
36	    }
37	  }
38	  return primaryLabel
39	})
40	
41	const ownerLayer = computed<"industry" | "public">(() => {
42	  if (!props.tool) return "public"
43	  return props.tool.ownerSecondary ? "public" : props.groups.find((g) => g.key === props.tool?.ownerPrimary)?.kind === "domain" ? "public" : "industry"
44	})
45	
46	const endpoint = computed(() =>
47	  props.tool && props.tool.status === "released"
48	    ? `mcp://${props.tool.slug}`
49	    : t("mcpPanorama.detail.notAvailable"),
50	)
51	
52	const downstreams = computed<ToolDto[]>(() => {
53	  if (!props.tool) return []
54	  // Deterministic pick from across the groups, capped at min(deps, 5) — purely
55	  // illustrative dependency list for the side panel. Walks the pool linearly
56	  // until n distinct tools are collected, so adjacent picks never collide.
57	  const all = props.groups.flatMap((g) => g.items).filter((x) => x.id !== props.tool!.id)
58	  if (all.length === 0) return []
59	  const target = Math.min(props.tool.depsCount, 5, all.length)
60	  const out: ToolDto[] = []
61	  const seen = new Set<number>()
62	  let i = props.tool.id * 7
63	  while (out.length < target) {
64	    const candidate = all[i % all.length]!
65	    if (!seen.has(candidate.id)) {
66	      seen.add(candidate.id)
67	      out.push(candidate)
68	    }
69	    i += 13
70	  }
71	  return out
72	})
73	</script>

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { groupDisplayTitle, rankFor, STATUS_ORDER, type Group } from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
3	
4	const props = defineProps<{ group: Group }>()
5	
6	const { locale, t } = useI18n()

13	    case "onTrack": return "bg-[oklch(95%_0.03_110)] text-[oklch(38%_0.07_110)] dark:bg-[oklch(22%_0.04_110)] dark:text-[oklch(72%_0.12_110)]"
14	    case "lagging": return "bg-[oklch(95%_0.04_50)] text-[oklch(40%_0.10_30)] dark:bg-[oklch(22%_0.04_30)] dark:text-[oklch(75%_0.14_30)]"
15	    case "early":   return "bg-(--color-status-none-bg) text-(--color-status-none)"
16	    default: return ""
17	  }
18	})
19	</script>
20	
21	<template>
22	  <header class="flex flex-col gap-2">

44	            :title="`${t(`mcpPanorama.status.${s}.label`)}: ${group.stats.counts[s]}`"
45	            :class="[
46	              'h-full',
47	              s === 'released' && 'bg-(--color-status-released)',
48	              s === 'dev' && 'bg-(--color-status-dev)',
49	              s === 'none' && 'bg-(--color-status-none) opacity-50',

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Menu, Search } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	defineProps<{ collapsed: boolean }>()
5	const emit = defineEmits<{ "toggle-sidebar": [] }>()
6	
7	const { t } = useI18n()
8	const route = useRoute()
9	const router = useRouter()
10	const localePath = useLocalePath()
11	
12	const q = ref<string>(typeof route.query.q === "string" ? route.query.q : "")
13	
14	watch(
15	  () => route.query.q,
16	  (next) => {
17	    q.value = typeof next === "string" ? next : ""
18	  },
19	)
20	
21	function onSubmit() {
22	  const query: Record<string, string> = {}
23	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
24	    if (typeof v === "string" && k !== "q" && k !== "page") query[k] = v
25	  }
26	  const trimmed = q.value.trim()
27	  if (trimmed) query.q = trimmed
28	  router.push({ path: localePath("/extensions"), query })
29	}
30	</script>
31

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/let-s-think-about-the-greedy-meteor.md",
  "content": "# MCP Panorama entry point + page polish\n\n## Context\n\nThe MCP Panorama landing shipped recently (commits `6870c2d`, docs `602f211`). Two issues surfaced when reviewing the result:\n\n1. **Entry point is poorly placed and styled.** `McpPanoramaBanner` only appears on `/extensions` when `category=mcp` is active — so most visitors never see it. Stylistically it's a small tinted notification strip wedged between the filter bar and the grid; it reads like a UI nudge, not a destination.\n2. **The panorama page is visually noisy in three specific places**:\n   - The `SectionHeader` search input duplicates the global search in `TopBar` (the layout renders `TopBar`, whose search field also lives at the top of the page).\n   - Each `CardHeader` renders a 5px stacked status bar **and** an inline dot+count legend **and** a percent suffix. Repeated across every card in the grid, the bar adds clutter without adding signal — the layer-level summary at the top of the page already shows the same proportions.\n   - The `ToolDetailPanel` slides in without a strong visual anchor, and its section hierarchy is flat (every section block is divided by the same hairline).\n\nGoal: make the entry point a deliberate, calm part of the marketplace and let the panorama page breathe.\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Replace the filter-gated banner with a top-of-page feature card\n\n- **Delete** `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue` and `McpPanoramaBanner.nuxt.test.ts`.\n- **Add** `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.vue` (and matching `.nuxt.test.ts`).\n- **Edit** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue:30-53`:\n  - Remove the `v-if=\"isMcpCategory\"` banner and the `isMcpCategory` computed (line 26).\n  - Render `<McpPanoramaFeature />` at the very top of the page (before the `<header>` block), unconditionally when `filters.value.q` is empty (suppress when the user is actively searching, so we don't compete with results).\n\n`McpPanoramaFeature.vue` design:\n- Two-column card on `--color-card` background with a subtle accent left-border (`border-l-2 border-(--color-accent)`), no full tint.\n- **Left**: serif eyebrow (`MCP PANORAMA`, mono uppercase, ink-muted), 22-24px serif title, single-line description, then a compact stat strip (e.g. `12 sectors · 240 tools · 2 layers`) in mono text. CTA at the bottom (chevron link).\n- **Right**: a quiet decorative motif — a small 3×N grid of dot/cell tiles colored with `--color-status-released / dev / none` at low opacity, evocative of the panorama grid itself. Pure CSS, no asset.\n- Stats sourced by reusing existing `/api/internal/mcp-landscape?layer=public` on the index page (already cached client-side) and deriving counts via `groups.length` + `layerStats.total`. No new endpoint.\n- Hide cleanly on small viewports: collapse decorative column, keep stat strip.\n\n### 2. Eliminate the duplicate search on the panorama page\n\n- **Edit** `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue:1-69`:\n  - Add `hideSearch?: boolean` to `defineProps`.\n  - Wrap the `<form role=\"search\">` block (lines 51-69) in `v-if=\"!hideSearch\"`. Keep TopBar's container layout stable by leaving a spacer when hidden.\n- **Edit** `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue:25`:\n  - Pass `:hide-search=\"true\"` to `<TopBar>`.\n- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue` search field (lines 159-171):\n  - Reframe it as a clearly-panorama filter: add a small mono caption above the input (`FILTER VISIBLE TOOLS` / `筛选可见工具`) and keep the input slightly narrower. Now visually distinct from a global search.\n\n### 3. Calm the SectionHeader into two tiers\n\n- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue:92-173`:\n  - **Tier 1** (title block, lines 94-139): breadcrumb + 36px serif title + 13px subtitle. Replace the \"Clear\" mono text button (lines 101-107) with a small `<X />` icon button next to the breadcrumb, `aria-label={t('mcpPanorama.filter.clear')}`.\n  - **Move the view toggle** (panorama/list, lines 115-138) out of Tier 1 and into Tier 2 as a small icon-only segmented control on the far right.\n  - **Tier 2** (controls, separated from Tier 1 with `border-t border-(--color-border) pt-4 mt-4`): status chips on the left, filter input in the middle (with its new caption), view toggle on the right.\n- Result: a clear title-then-controls rhythm; no more flex-wrap collision between chips and search.\n\n### 4. Drop the progress bar from each card header\n\n- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/CardHeader.vue:39-76`:\n  - Remove the 5px stacked status bar (lines 40-54).\n  - Keep the inline dot+count legend (lines 56-71) as a single tight row.\n  - Drop the percent suffix (lines 72-74) — the rank pill already encodes the leading/onTrack/lagging/early signal.\n- Net effect across the grid: each card header is title + count + rank pill + a one-line legend. Much less visual repetition. The page-level proportions stay visible in `LayerSummary` at the top.\n\n### 5. Polish the ToolDetailPanel\n\n- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolDetailPanel.vue`:\n  - **Anchor**: add `border-l-2 border-(--color-accent)` to the `<aside>` (replacing or complementing the existing `border-l border-(--color-border)`) when `open` — gives the panel a stronger seam against the main content.\n  - **Close button** (lines 101-108): increase hit target to `p-2`, add `hover:bg-(--color-sidebar) hover:text-(--color-ink)`, keep keyboard focus ring.\n  - **Header rhythm** (lines 83-111): move the `StatusPill` from the Status block (line 119) into the header row next to the layer chip, so the body of the Status block becomes a single 13px description paragraph. One fewer label/value row in the panel.\n  - **Meta grid** (lines 127-148): bump label size from `text-[10px]` to `text-[11px]` for legibility; bump `gap-y-3.5` to `gap-y-4`.\n  - **Downstream list** (lines 151-175): keep current structure; add `font-mono text-[10px] text-(--color-ink-muted)` leading bullet (`·`) before each row title to differentiate from the meta grid visually.\n\n## Files touched\n\n- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — swap banner for feature card at top\n- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue` — **delete**\n- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.nuxt.test.ts` — **delete**\n- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.vue` — **new**\n- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.nuxt.test.ts` — **new**\n- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — accept `hideSearch` prop\n- `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue` — pass `:hide-search=\"true\"`\n- `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue` — two-tier reorg, X close button, view toggle moved into Tier 2, filter caption\n- `app/components/mcp-landscape/CardHeader.vue` — remove status bar + percent suffix\n- `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolDetailPanel.vue` — accent seam, status pill moved into header, label legibility, downstream bullets\n- `app/i18n/locales/en.json` + `zh.json` — new keys: `extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.{eyebrow,title,description,sectors,tools,layers,cta}`, `mcpPanorama.filter.filterCaption`. Remove `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.*`.\n\n## Verification\n\nRun `bun run dev` and walk through:\n\n- `/extensions` → feature card visible at the top with stats; no banner between filter bar and grid.\n- `/extensions?q=foo` → feature card hidden (active search).\n- `/extensions?category=mcp` → still no banner; feature card shown (assuming no `q`).\n- `/mcp-panorama` → global search in TopBar is gone; SectionHeader has clean two-tier layout; status chips and the filter input don't wrap into each other; clicking the X next to the breadcrumb clears the drill-down.\n- Click any card → cards now have a single-row status legend (no 5px bar, no percent suffix).\n- Open detail panel → accent left edge visible; status pill in header (not a separate row); close button has visible hover state.\n- Switch locale to `zh` → all new strings render.\n- Toggle dark theme → feature card, simplified card header, and detail panel maintain contrast.\n\nThen:\n\n- `bun run test` — `McpPanoramaFeature` tests pass; verify `CardHeader` rendering test (if any) is updated for the removed bar.\n- `bun run lint && bun run typecheck`.\n\n## Out of scope (call out for follow-up)\n\n- `LayerSummary` and `SectorCard`/`DomainCard` body layouts beyond `CardHeader` — kept untouched per the tight-on-cards direction; revisit if the simpler card headers reveal new imbalance.\n- Backend/payload changes — using existing landscape endpoint for the feature-card stats.\n- The `mcp-panorama` layout chrome (header height, sidebar widths) — unchanged.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# MCP Panorama entry point + page polish

## Context

The MCP Panorama landing shipped recently (commits `6870c2d`, docs `602f211`). Two issues surfaced when reviewing the result:

1. **Entry point is poorly placed and styled.** `McpPanoramaBanner` only appears on `/extensions` when `category=mcp` is active — so most visitors never see it. Stylistically it's a small tinted notification strip wedged between the filter bar and the grid; it reads like a UI nudge, not a destination.
2. **The panorama page is visually noisy in three specific places**:
   - The `SectionHeader` search input duplicates the global search in `TopBar` (the layout renders `TopBar`, whose search field also lives at the top of the page).
   - Each `CardHeader` renders a 5px stacked status bar **and** an inline dot+count legend **and** a percent suffix. Repeated across every card in the grid, the bar adds clutter without adding signal — the layer-level summary at the top of the page already shows the same proportions.
   - The `ToolDetailPanel` slides in without a strong visual anchor, and its section hierarchy is flat (every section block is divided by the same hairline).

Goal: make the entry point a deliberate, calm part of the marketplace and let the panorama page breathe.

## Changes

### 1. Replace the filter-gated banner with a top-of-page feature card

- **Delete** `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue` and `McpPanoramaBanner.nuxt.test.ts`.
- **Add** `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.vue` (and matching `.nuxt.test.ts`).
- **Edit** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue:30-53`:
  - Remove the `v-if="isMcpCategory"` banner and the `isMcpCategory` computed (line 26).
  - Render `<McpPanoramaFeature />` at the very top of the page (before the `<header>` block), unconditionally when `filters.value.q` is empty (suppress when the user is actively searching, so we don't compete with results).

`McpPanoramaFeature.vue` design:
- Two-column card on `--color-card` background with a subtle accent left-border (`border-l-2 border-(--color-accent)`), no full tint.
- **Left**: serif eyebrow (`MCP PANORAMA`, mono uppercase, ink-muted), 22-24px serif title, single-line description, then a compact stat strip (e.g. `12 sectors · 240 tools · 2 layers`) in mono text. CTA at the bottom (chevron link).
- **Right**: a quiet decorative motif — a small 3×N grid of dot/cell tiles colored with `--color-status-released / dev / none` at low opacity, evocative of the panorama grid itself. Pure CSS, no asset.
- Stats sourced by reusing existing `/api/internal/mcp-landscape?layer=public` on the index page (already cached client-side) and deriving counts via `groups.length` + `layerStats.total`. No new endpoint.
- Hide cleanly on small viewports: collapse decorative column, keep stat strip.

### 2. Eliminate the duplicate search on the panorama page

- **Edit** `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue:1-69`:
  - Add `hideSearch?: boolean` to `defineProps`.
  - Wrap the `<form role="search">` block (lines 51-69) in `v-if="!hideSearch"`. Keep TopBar's container layout stable by leaving a spacer when hidden.
- **Edit** `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue:25`:
  - Pass `:hide-search="true"` to `<TopBar>`.
- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue` search field (lines 159-171):
  - Reframe it as a clearly-panorama filter: add a small mono caption above the input (`FILTER VISIBLE TOOLS` / `筛选可见工具`) and keep the input slightly narrower. Now visually distinct from a global search.

### 3. Calm the SectionHeader into two tiers

- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue:92-173`:
  - **Tier 1** (title block, lines 94-139): breadcrumb + 36px serif title + 13px subtitle. Replace the "Clear" mono text button (lines 101-107) with a small `<X />` icon button next to the breadcrumb, `aria-label={t('mcpPanorama.filter.clear')}`.
  - **Move the view toggle** (panorama/list, lines 115-138) out of Tier 1 and into Tier 2 as a small icon-only segmented control on the far right.
  - **Tier 2** (controls, separated from Tier 1 with `border-t border-(--color-border) pt-4 mt-4`): status chips on the left, filter input in the middle (with its new caption), view toggle on the right.
- Result: a clear title-then-controls rhythm; no more flex-wrap collision between chips and search.

### 4. Drop the progress bar from each card header

- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/CardHeader.vue:39-76`:
  - Remove the 5px stacked status bar (lines 40-54).
  - Keep the inline dot+count legend (lines 56-71) as a single tight row.
  - Drop the percent suffix (lines 72-74) — the rank pill already encodes the leading/onTrack/lagging/early signal.
- Net effect across the grid: each card header is title + count + rank pill + a one-line legend. Much less visual repetition. The page-level proportions stay visible in `LayerSummary` at the top.

### 5. Polish the ToolDetailPanel

- **Edit** `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolDetailPanel.vue`:
  - **Anchor**: add `border-l-2 border-(--color-accent)` to the `<aside>` (replacing or complementing the existing `border-l border-(--color-border)`) when `open` — gives the panel a stronger seam against the main content.
  - **Close button** (lines 101-108): increase hit target to `p-2`, add `hover:bg-(--color-sidebar) hover:text-(--color-ink)`, keep keyboard focus ring.
  - **Header rhythm** (lines 83-111): move the `StatusPill` from the Status block (line 119) into the header row next to the layer chip, so the body of the Status block becomes a single 13px description paragraph. One fewer label/value row in the panel.
  - **Meta grid** (lines 127-148): bump label size from `text-[10px]` to `text-[11px]` for legibility; bump `gap-y-3.5` to `gap-y-4`.
  - **Downstream list** (lines 151-175): keep current structure; add `font-mono text-[10px] text-(--color-ink-muted)` leading bullet (`·`) before each row title to differentiate from the meta grid visually.

## Files touched

- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — swap banner for feature card at top
- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.vue` — **delete**
- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaBanner.nuxt.test.ts` — **delete**
- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.vue` — **new**
- `app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.nuxt.test.ts` — **new**
- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — accept `hideSearch` prop
- `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue` — pass `:hide-search="true"`
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue` — two-tier reorg, X close button, view toggle moved into Tier 2, filter caption
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/CardHeader.vue` — remove status bar + percent suffix
- `app/components/mcp-landscape/ToolDetailPanel.vue` — accent seam, status pill moved into header, label legibility, downstream bullets
- `app/i18n/locales/en.json` + `zh.json` — new keys: `extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.{eyebrow,title,description,sectors,tools,layers,cta}`, `mcpPanorama.filter.filterCaption`. Remove `extensions.mcpPanoramaBanner.*`.

## Verification

Run `bun run dev` and walk through:

- `/extensions` → feature card visible at the top with stats; no banner between filter bar and grid.
- `/extensions?q=foo` → feature card hidden (active search).
- `/extensions?category=mcp` → still no banner; feature card shown (assuming no `q`).
- `/mcp-panorama` → global search in TopBar is gone; SectionHeader has clean two-tier layout; status chips and the filter input don't wrap into each other; clicking the X next to the breadcrumb clears the drill-down.
- Click any card → cards now have a single-row status legend (no 5px bar, no percent suffix).
- Open detail panel → accent left edge visible; status pill in header (not a separate row); close button has visible hover state.
- Switch locale to `zh` → all new strings render.
- Toggle dark theme → feature card, simplified card header, and detail panel maintain contrast.

Then:

- `bun run test` — `McpPanoramaFeature` tests pass; verify `CardHeader` rendering test (if any) is updated for the removed bar.
- `bun run lint && bun run typecheck`.

## Out of scope (call out for follow-up)

- `LayerSummary` and `SectorCard`/`DomainCard` body layouts beyond `CardHeader` — kept untouched per the tight-on-cards direction; revisit if the simpler card headers reveal new imbalance.
- Backend/payload changes — using existing landscape endpoint for the feature-card stats.
- The `mcp-panorama` layout chrome (header height, sidebar widths) — unchanged.

```

---shared mcp-panorama types---
// Types and helpers shared between the MCP Panorama API and its UI.

import type { McpStatus } from "~~/shared/data/mcp-landscape"

export type Layer = "industry" | "public"

export interface ToolDto {
  id: number
  slug: string
  name: string
  nameZh: string | null
  status: McpStatus
  depsCount: number
  blurb: string
  blurbZh: string
  tags: string[]
  /** When status === "released", the marketplace listing slug. */
  extensionSlug: string | null
  ownerPrimary: string
  ownerSecondary: string | null
}

export interface StatusCounts {
  released: number
  dev: number
  none: number
}

export interface GroupStats {
  total: number
  counts: StatusCounts
  releasedPct: number
  activePct: number
  lagPct: number
}

export interface PdtBlock {
  key: string
  label: string
  labelZh: string
  items: ToolDto[]
}

export interface SectorGroup {
  kind: "sector"
  key: string
  label: string
  labelZh: string
  short: string
  items: ToolDto[]
  stats: GroupStats
}

export interface DomainGroup {
  kind: "domain"
  key: string
  label: string
  labelZh: string
  short: string
  items: ToolDto[]
  pdts: PdtBlock[]
  stats: GroupStats
}

export type Group = SectorGroup | DomainGroup

export interface LayerPayload {
  layer: Layer
  layerStats: GroupStats
  groups: Group[]
}

export type RankKey = "leading" | "onTrack" | "lagging" | "early"

/** Returns the rank label for a group's stats — null if too small or middling. */
export function rankFor(stats: GroupStats): RankKey | null {
  if (stats.total < 3) return null
  if (stats.releasedPct >= 75) return "leading"
  if (stats.releasedPct >= 50) return "onTrack"
  if (stats.lagPct >= 50) return "lagging"
  if (stats.releasedPct < 25) return "early"
  return null
}

export const STATUS_ORDER: McpStatus[] = ["none", "dev", "released"]

/** Localised display name for a tool (Chinese fallback to English when null). */
export function toolDisplayName(tool: ToolDto, locale: string): string {
  if (locale === "zh" && tool.nameZh) return tool.nameZh
  return tool.name
}

/** Localised display blurb. */
export function toolDisplayBlurb(tool: ToolDto, locale: string): string {
  return locale === "zh" ? tool.blurbZh : tool.blurb
}

/** Localised group title. */
export function groupDisplayTitle(g: Group, locale: string): string {
  return locale === "zh" ? g.labelZh : g.label
```

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ArrowUpRight } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\nimport type { LayerPayload } from \"~~/shared/mcp-panorama\"\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\n// Same key as the panorama page uses, so this dedupes when the user\n// later navigates over.\nconst { data } = await useFetch<LayerPayload>(\"/api/internal/mcp-landscape\", {\n  query: { layer: \"public\" },\n  key: \"mcp-landscape\",\n  default: () => null,\n})\n\nconst stats = computed(() => {\n  if (!data.value) return null\n  return {\n    domains: data.value.groups.length,\n    tools: data.value.layerStats.total,\n  }\n})\n\n// Decorative motif: a fixed 4x6 grid of cells using the three status tokens\n// at low opacity. Deterministic pattern, no randomness — purely visual.\nconst motif = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => {\n  const r = (i * 7 + 3) % 11\n  if (r < 5) return \"released\"\n  if (r < 8) return \"dev\"\n  return \"none\"\n})\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <NuxtLink\n    :to=\"localePath('/mcp-panorama')\"\n    class=\"group relative block mb-7 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-(--color-border) border-l-2 border-l-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-card) transition hover:border-(--color-accent)/50\"\n  >\n    <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-6 px-6 py-6 md:flex-row md:items-center md:gap-8 md:px-8 md:py-7\">\n      <div class=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n        <div class=\"font-mono text-[11px] tracking-[0.12em] text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n          {{ t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.eyebrow\") }}\n        </div>\n        <h2 class=\"mt-2 font-serif text-[24px] md:text-[26px] font-medium tracking-tight text-(--color-ink) leading-[1.15]\">\n          {{ t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.title\") }}\n        </h2>\n        <p class=\"mt-2 max-w-2xl text-[14px] text-(--color-ink-muted) leading-snug\">\n          {{ t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.description\") }}\n        </p>\n        <div class=\"mt-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap\">\n          <span class=\"font-mono text-[12px] text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n            {{ stats\n              ? t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.stats\", stats)\n              : t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.statsFallback\") }}\n          </span>\n          <span class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-(--color-accent) transition group-hover:gap-2.5\">\n            {{ t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaFeature.cta\") }}\n            <ArrowUpRight :size=\"14\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div\n        class=\"hidden md:grid shrink-0 grid-cols-6 gap-1.5 self-stretch items-center\"\n        aria-hidden=\"true\"\n      >\n        <span\n          v-for=\"(s, i) in motif\"\n          :key=\"i\"\n          class=\"size-3 rounded-[3px]\"\n          :class=\"[\n            s === 'released' && 'bg-(--color-status-released)/55',\n            s === 'dev' && 'bg-(--color-status-dev)/45',\n            s === 'none' && 'bg-(--color-ink-muted)/20',\n          ]\"\n        />\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </NuxtLink>\n</template>\n</content>\n</invoke>"
}

> AGENT

Committed locally on `feat/mcp-panorama-polish` (commit `01a35d1`). Not pushed.

**What changed**
- New `McpPanoramaFeature.vue` (+ test) replaces the filter-gated `McpPanoramaBanner` and renders at the top of `/extensions` whenever no search is active — with eyebrow, serif title, description, live stats (`{domains} domains · {tools} tools · 2 layers`), and a decorative motif using status tokens.
- TopBar grows a `hideSearch` prop; the `mcp-panorama` layout passes `true` so the global search no longer competes with the in-page filter.
- `SectionHeader` is now a two-tier layout: title (breadcrumb + ✕ icon + 36px serif title + subtitle), then a hairline separator, then controls (status chips left, filter input + icon-only view toggle right). Filter input carries a `FILTER VISIBLE TOOLS` caption.
- `CardHeader` drops the 5px stacked status bar and the percent suffix — keeps the inline dot+count legend.
- `ToolDetailPanel` gets an accent left seam when open, a larger close hit target with hover, the status pill moved up into the header row, bumped meta-label sizes, and leading bullets on the downstream rows.

**Verification**
- `bun run lint` clean, `bun run typecheck` clean, `bun run test` → 236/236 passing (incl. new feature-card tests).
- `bun run dev` walkthrough confirmed: feature card appears on `/extensions` and `/extensions?category=mcp`, hides on `/extensions?q=test`; TopBar `topbar-search` input is absent from `/mcp-panorama` but present on `/extensions`.

Let me know when you've reviewed and I'll push (or amend/split the commit if you'd like it reorganized first).

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { LayoutGrid, List, Search, X } from "lucide-vue-next"

4	import {
5	  groupDisplayTitle,
6	  pdtDisplayTitle,
7	  STATUS_ORDER,
8	  type Group,
9	  type Layer,
10	  type PdtBlock,
11	} from "~~/shared/mcp-panorama"
12	import StatusChip from "./StatusChip.vue"
13	
14	const props = defineProps<{
15	  layer: Layer
16	  activePrimary: string | null
17	  activeSecondary: string | null
18	  visibleCounts: { total: number; released: number; dev: number; none: number }
19	  totals: { total: number }
20	  statusFilter: "all" | McpStatus
21	  viewMode: "panorama" | "list"
22	  search: string
23	  groups: Group[]
24	}>()
25	
26	const emit = defineEmits<{
27	  "update:statusFilter": ["all" | McpStatus]
28	  "update:viewMode": ["panorama" | "list"]
29	  "update:search": [string]
30	  clearDrill: []
31	}>()
32	
33	const { locale, t } = useI18n()
34	
35	const layerLabel = computed(() => t(`mcpPanorama.layer.${props.layer}`))
36	
37	const titleAndCrumb = computed<{ title: string; crumb: string | null }>(() => {
38	  if (!props.activePrimary) {
39	    return { title: t("mcpPanorama.layer." + props.layer), crumb: null }
40	  }
41	  const primary = props.groups.find((g) => g.key === props.activePrimary)
42	  if (!primary) return { title: props.activePrimary, crumb: layerLabel.value }
43	  if (props.layer === "industry") {
44	    return {
45	      title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value),
46	      crumb: layerLabel.value,
47	    }
48	  }
49	  if (primary.kind !== "domain") {
50	    return { title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value), crumb: layerLabel.value }
51	  }
52	  if (props.activeSecondary) {
53	    const pdt = primary.pdts.find((p) => p.key === props.activeSecondary) as PdtBlock | undefined
54	    if (pdt) {
55	      return {
56	        title: pdtDisplayTitle(pdt, locale.value),
57	        crumb: `${layerLabel.value} / ${groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value)}`,
58	      }
59	    }
60	  }
61	  return {
62	    title: groupDisplayTitle(primary, locale.value),
63	    crumb: layerLabel.value,
64	  }
65	})
66	
67	const subtitle = computed(() => {
68	  if (!props.activePrimary) {
69	    return t("mcpPanorama.page.subtitleAll", {
70	      visible: props.visibleCounts.total,
71	      total: props.totals.total,
72	    })
73	  }
74	  return t("mcpPanorama.page.subtitleScoped", {
75	    visible: props.visibleCounts.total,
76	    released: props.visibleCounts.released,
77	    dev: props.visibleCounts.dev,
78	    none: props.visibleCounts.none,
79	  })
80	})
81	
82	function statusLabel(s: McpStatus): string {
83	  return t(`mcpPanorama.status.${s}.label`)
84	}
85	
86	const localSearch = computed({
87	  get: () => props.search,
88	  set: (v: string) => emit("update:search", v),
89	})
90	</script>

**Migrate icon-only buttons to the Button component.**

The clear button (lines 94–101) and view-toggle buttons (lines 132–155) are raw `<button>` elements. Refactor these to use the project's `Button` component with `size="icon"` to match the established UI pattern.

<details>
<summary>Example refactor</summary>

```vue
<!-- Before: raw button -->
<button
  type="button"
  class="inline-flex items-center justify-center size-[18px] rounded-full text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-border)/60 cursor-pointer transition"
  :aria-label="t('mcpPanorama.filter.clear')"
  `@click`="emit('clearDrill')"
>
  <X :size="11" aria-hidden="true" />
</button>

<!-- After: Button component -->
<Button size="icon" variant="ghost" :aria-label="t('mcpPanorama.filter.clear')" `@click`="emit('clearDrill')">
  <X :size="11" aria-hidden="true" />
</Button>
```

</details>

Per coding guidelines, `app/components/**/*.vue` must use Reka UI + Button component primitives for all interactive controls.

`39-45`: _⚡ Quick win_

**Consider explicit locale setup in tests.**

The test assertions rely on hardcoded English strings. If the test environment locale changes or translations are updated, the tests may fail unexpectedly. Consider either explicitly setting the locale in test setup or using i18n key lookups with expected translations.


<details>
<summary>💡 Example: Explicit locale configuration</summary>

```typescript
// In test setup or describe block
const wrapper = await mountSuspended(McpPanoramaFeature, {
  ...mountOpts,
  global: {
    ...mountOpts.global,
    mocks: {
      $i18n: { locale: 'en' }
    }
  }
})
```

Alternatively, mock the i18n responses to decouple tests from translation file changes.

Here's the review on PR #21 — one human-review-equivalent reviewer (CodeRabbit). CI `validate` is green, Vercel preview deployed.

**4 actionable comments (and 1 nitpick):**

1. **`app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.vue:24`** (🟡 minor) — Comment says "4x6 grid" but the template uses `grid-cols-6` with 24 cells, i.e. 6 columns × 4 rows. Fix the comment wording. *Valid — my comment is wrong.*

2. **`app/components/mcp-landscape/CardHeader.vue:50-53`** (🟡 minor) — The `none` chip shows dot + count only while the other two chips have a text label. Suggests adding `mcpPanorama.card.none` (which already exists in i18n — the EN value is `"—"`, ZH is `"—"`). *Worth checking — when I removed the percent suffix I left the none chip without a label for asymmetry. CodeRabbit's claim that "color alone" carries meaning is technically true; adding a label could either help or feel busy. Judgment call.*

3. **`app/components/mcp-landscape/SectionHeader.vue:94-101, 132-155`** (🟠 major) — The clear-drill `<X>` button and the view-toggle `<button>` pair are raw `<button>` elements. Per "coding guidelines" referenced by the reviewer, components should use the project's `Button` component (Reka UI primitive with `size="icon"` / `variant="ghost"`). *Worth verifying — the existing `SectionHeader` already had raw buttons before this PR; the reviewer is suggesting a consistency tightening. Look at how `Button.vue` is currently used elsewhere to judge whether this is genuinely the house style.*

4. **`i18n/locales/zh.json:533`** (🟡 minor) — The `clear` Chinese string is `"清除筛选"` ("clear filters", suggests global reset) but the action only resets drill-down. Suggested wording: `"清除下钻"` / `"清除当前筛选"`. *Valid — I changed the EN copy to "Clear drill-down" but left ZH as "清除筛选". They should match.*

**1 nitpick:**

5. **`McpPanoramaFeature.nuxt.test.ts:39-45`** — Tests assert hardcoded EN strings; suggests mocking i18n. *Probably overkill — the existing banner test did the same thing (and so do many tests in this repo).*

**Vercel automated comments:** preview deployment Ready at https://agentcenter-nuxt-git-feat-mcp-panorama-polish-hutusis-projects.vercel.app — no human-flagged issues. CodeRabbit's main walkthrough comment is just a summary, not feedback.

**My take on what's worth acting on:** #1 (comment fix), #4 (ZH copy alignment), and likely #3 (Button component consistency) are the substantive ones. #2 is debatable — the asymmetry was deliberate but a label wouldn't hurt. #5 I'd skip. Want me to address them?

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { HTMLAttributes } from "vue"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { Primitive, type PrimitiveProps } from "reka-ui"
import { cn } from "~/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)/40 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg) hover:bg-(--color-accent)/90",
        outline: "border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)",
        ghost: "text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)",
        secondary: "bg-(--color-sidebar) text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)/80 border border-(--color-border)",
        destructive: "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700",
        link: "text-(--color-accent) underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-6",
        icon: "size-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default", size: "default" },
  },
)

export type ButtonVariants = VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants>

const props = withDefaults(
  defineProps<PrimitiveProps & {
    variant?: ButtonVariants["variant"]
    size?: ButtonVariants["size"]
    class?: HTMLAttributes["class"]
  }>(),
  { as: "button" },
)
</script>

<template>
  <Primitive
    :as="as"
    :as-child="asChild"
    :class="cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), props.class)"
  >
    <slot />
  </Primitive>
</template>

/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/docs/plan.md:586:**`exactOptionalPropertyTypes` was tried and dropped in P15.** Vue's `defineProps` adds explicit `undefined` to optional prop types, and reka-ui's prop-forwarding helpers (`useForwardProps`, `useForwardPropsEmits`) then route those values into reka-ui's stricter optional props, which refuse explicit `undefined`. The result is dozens of `TS2379` errors at every shadcn-vue forwarding boundary. The fix would be either casting at each forward site or relaxing the flag globally — the former is verbose and ts-ignorant; the latter loses the "JSON-serializer-shape-drift" guarantee that motivated the flag. Since the marketplace doesn't have a hot JSON-shape-drift path that this flag was preventing, the trade favoured relaxing.

> AGENT

Quick re-evaluation of #3 before touching it. Raw `<button>` is the dominant pattern in this codebase — every file under `app/components/mcp-landscape/`, `app/components/filters/`, layout components, and `extension/InstallButton.vue` all use raw `<button>`. The `Button` component is reserved for form-style controls in `publish/` and `profile/` wizards. CLAUDE.md and `docs/plan.md` don't impose a "must use Button" rule for inline controls.

CodeRabbit's "Per coding guidelines, `app/components/**/*.vue` must use Reka UI + Button primitives for all interactive controls" appears to be pattern-matched from generic shadcn projects, not this codebase. Skipping #3 — keeping consistency with sibling files (`StatusChip.vue`, `LayerSidebar.vue`, `ToolDetailPanel.vue`, etc. all use raw `<button>`).

Applying #1 and #4 only.
